headline: Will Wegovy Pill Drive Renewed GLP-1 Growth Momentum for Novo Stock?
<<<SUMMARY>>>
Novo's newly launched Wegovy oral pill generated DKK 5.4 billion in Q3 2026 sales with 3.6 million U.S. prescriptions, prompting the company to raise its 2026 growth outlook. However, the company faces intense competition from Eli Lilly's surging Mounjaro and Zepbound sales, as well as emerging competitors like Viking Therapeutics and Structure Therapeutics developing alternative GLP-1 therapies. Novo's stock has underperformed the industry year-to-date, trading at a discount valuation.
<<<INSIGHTS>>>
NVO | positive | Strong Q3 sales of Wegovy pill (DKK 5.4B), exceeded 8M prescriptions since launch, raised 2026 growth outlook, maintains >50% market share in U.S. branded obesity market, and expanding international footprint support positive momentum.
LLY | positive | Mounjaro sales surged 65% YoY to $10.7B, Zepbound sales rose 49% to $5.4B, Foundayo generated $184M in early sales, and achieved 39% overall revenue growth, demonstrating formidable competitive strength in GLP-1 market.
VKTX | neutral | Advancing VK2735 dual GIPR/GLP-1 RA in phase III trials with both oral and subcutaneous formulations, representing emerging competition but still in development stage with results expected in 2027.
GPCR | neutral | Developing aleniglipron oral GLP-1 agonist in phase III ACCOMPLISH program with top-line data expected in H2 2028, representing future competitive threat but currently in early-stage development.
